4040 — landmine rear lunge v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4040 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Reverse lunge holding the end of a landmine bar at your chest. The bar adds load and encourages an upright torso.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold the bar end against your chest with both hands. Stand tall with feet hip-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4040.json#/instructions/0, 4040.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the bar end against your chest with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4040.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand tall with feet hip-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4040.json#/instructions/1

For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4040.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Hold the bar end against your chest with both hands. 4040.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand tall with feet hip-width apart. 4040.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step one foot back and lower until both knees are bent about 90 degrees. 4040.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your front heel down and your torso upright. 4040.json#/instructions/3
  5. Push through the front foot to return, then switch legs. 4040.json#/instructions/4

4041 — dumbbell russian twist (on stability ball)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4041 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Rotating a dumbbell side to side while balanced on a stability ball. It challenges the obliques and balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a stability ball and walk your feet out until your upper back rests on it, hips lifted. Hold a dumbbell with both hands above your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4041.json#/instructions/0, 4041.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold a dumbbell with both hands above your chest.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4041.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a stability ball and walk your feet out until your upper back rests on it, hips lifted. 4041.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold a dumbbell with both hands above your chest. 4041.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your torso and arms to one side. 4041.json#/instructions/2
  4. Rotate smoothly to the other side. 4041.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your hips high and move with control. 4041.json#/instructions/4

4042 — cable seated cross arm twist

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4042 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Seated trunk rotation pulling a cable across the body. It works the obliques and deep core.

Proposed staging

4043 — inverted shrug (on parallel bars)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4043 after decompression) · Source record

Hanging from parallel bars, you shrug your shoulder blades up and down. It builds the traps and shoulder control.

Proposed staging

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Grip the parallel bars and hang in an inverted position with arms straight. 4043.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your body stable and your elbows locked. 4043.json#/instructions/1
  3. Draw your shoulder blades down and together, pulling your body up a little. 4043.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower back to a full hang under control. 4043.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for controlled reps. 4043.json#/instructions/4

4044 — single leg glute bridge with knee to chest

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4044 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

One leg glute bridge where the free knee is hugged toward the chest. It strengthens the glutes and challenges hip stability.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with one knee bent and the foot flat, the other knee pulled toward your chest. Brace your core.
